Business compatibility content
121 groups, 35 categories, 190 roles. Every group has its own Question 1 and Question 2 text for four match levels.
Placeholders in square brackets are filled by the app from the selected category and role.
How the score works
Top five categories in each group keep the scores from your source file. All other categories and every role get a fixed score
from the same formula, so the same input always gives the same result. Levels: 80 and above strong, 65 to 79 good, 50 to 64
uphill, below 50 not really worth it. Question 1 shows two roles only: the one selected and the role in the same category with
the nearest score. The best options in Question 2 come from the five curated categories of each group.
Placeholders
[role] [score] [demand1] [demand2] [optionA] [optionB] [optionC] [roleA] [roleB] [roleC]. Options skip the category the user
selected.
Worked example (resolved text, as the user sees it)
Selected: Rental agency in Real Estate. Score: 56%. Verdict: Not an easy fit.
Role
Fit
Why
Rental agency
56%
You can grow into selling over time
Real estate agency
55%
Selling is workable with some practice
Question 1. Your result of 56% says Rental agency will not come easily to you. Technical skills, leadership and selling skills
are where you are at your best. Rental agency asks for selling and client communication, plus tasks that do not match your
strengths. Cover patience with people and money sense with a partner before you spend much. You can still do it. Keep it as a
side income, bring in a partner for the weaker areas, and review your numbers after 60 days. You can also join our 2 months
business growth plan.
Question 2. With Rental agency at 56%, look closely at Construction, Chemicals, Metals & Machinery and Sports &
Recreation, where your strengths count for more. Inside those fields, Plumbing services, Welding shop and Sports academy
are good places to start. The abilities that will help you grow most are technical skills, leadership and selling skills. Plan support
for patience with people and money sense. Move your main effort to Construction and keep Rental agency as a small side
project. Review it after 60 days, or join our 2 months business growth plan.
Good at: technical skills; leadership; selling skills. Needs support in: patience with people; money sense.
These results are general guidance based on working style strengths. They are not a promise of business success.

Question 2: Best options for your growth
Strong match (80 and above)
[role] at [score]% is a strong base. Beyond it, your best fields are [optionA], [optionB] and [optionC]. If you want a role to aim
for, try [roleA], [roleB] or [roleC]. You are strongest in technical skills, leadership and selling skills, and you will want backup for

income.
Good match (65 to 79)
A score of [score]% means [role] is possible. You may do even better in [optionA], [optionB] or [optionC]. For specific roles,
consider [roleA], [roleB] or [roleC]. Your growth will come from technical skills, leadership and selling skills. Keep patience with
people and money sense covered by someone you trust. Run [role] carefully, and let [optionA] be your plan if it grows slowly.
